{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\nFollow this workflow; do not treat a green build or successful upload as proof that the intended app shipped.\n\n## Start with the requested stack\n\nWhen the user wants Scaffold-ETH 2 or has not selected a stack for a conventional contract-plus-frontend dApp, use its generator:\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@2.0.23 # Tested version; update deliberately\n```\n\nUse the generated Foundry/Next.js monorepo, wallet integration, contract hooks, and components. Respect an explicitly requested alternative stack.\n\n## Choose the local chain deliberately\n\n- Use `yarn chain` for isolated contracts, mocks, and unit tests.\n- Use `yarn fork --network <chain>` when behavior depends on deployed protocols, tokens, balances, or other real chain state.\n- In fork mode, point the frontend at the local Anvil network (`chains.foundry`, chain ID 31337), not the upstream chain being copied. Switch to the real target chain only for a real deployment.\n\nA fork also grants powers the real chain does not. Fund demo identities from state that already exists rather than deploying a mock token:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount <whale>\ncast send <token> \"transfer(address,uint256)\" <demo-account> <amount> \\\n  --from <whale> --unlocked\n```\n\n`anvil_setBalance` and `anvil_setStorageAt` are equivalent overrides when no suitable holder exists. Nothing here is broadcast; the fork is a local copy.\n\nAnvil normally mines only when a transaction arrives, so between transactions the latest block and `block.timestamp` remain frozen; the next transaction advances time in one jump. This silently breaks live deadlines, expiry, and vesting displays even when `vm.warp` unit tests pass. For continuous behavior, enable interval mining:\n\n```bash\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nFor a frozen live demo, enable interval mining. Mining a single block with `evm_mine` restamps `block.timestamp` once and it freezes again immediately; manual mining and `evm_increaseTime` are for controlled single-step tests, not a running demo. Add `--block-time 1` to the fork script when the project should always run that way.\n\n## Build a static IPFS release\n\nConfigure the IPFS build for static export, route directories, and static images:\n\n```typescript\nif (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\") {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\nSet the production origin before building so Open Graph URLs and images do not resolve to localhost. Then remove old artifacts and rebuild:\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://<production-domain>\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\nOn Node 25, the built-in `localStorage` global can exist without the standard Web Storage methods when no backing file is configured; libraries detect it and crash on calls such as `getItem()` during static prerender. Apply a process-level remedy inherited by build workers, not only code in `instrumentation.ts` or `next.config.ts`. Depending on the app, use one of:\n\n```bash\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--localstorage-file=.node-localstorage\"\nNODE_OPTIONS=\"--no-experimental-webstorage\"\n```\n\n## Verify before and after upload\n\nBefore uploading, confirm the expected change is in `out/`, that every route emitted its own directory, and that generated metadata carries the production origin:\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html # One directory with an index.html per route\n```\n\nAfter uploading:\n\n1. Record the CID. An unchanged CID means the uploaded bytes are identical; check the build output and upload target before blaming gateway caching.\n2. Load the root and at least one non-home route through the gateway. Root success does not prove exported routes resolve.\n\n   ```bash\n   curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://<gateway>/ipfs/<cid>/debug/\" # Expect 200, not 404\n   ```\n\n3. Verify the Open Graph image and URL use the production origin.\n4. For ENS, update the content hash only after the deployed CID has been reviewed and approved, then confirm the resolver and public gateway serve that CID.\n\nDo not claim the release is complete until these checks pass.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"09f64480-2f1a-49ab-84e6-a79d157cfa5b","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:23:28.080Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y9UALSg7RsVu56MR8hQ3JV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\t// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t  // State Variables\n17\t  address public immutable owner;\n18\t  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t  bool public premium = false;\n20\t  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t  event GreetingChange(\n25\t    address indexed greetingSetter,\n26\t    string newGreeting,\n27\t    bool premium,\n28\t    uint256 value\n29\t  );\n30\t\n31\t  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n32\t  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n33\t  constructor(address _owner) {\n34\t    owner = _owner;\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n38\t  // Check the withdraw() function\n39\t  modifier isOwner() {\n40\t    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n41\t    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n42\t    _;\n43\t  }\n44\t\n45\t  /**\n46\t   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n47\t   *\n48\t   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n49\t   */\n50\t  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n51\t    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n52\t\n53\t    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n54\t    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n55\t\n56\t    greeting = _newGreeting;\n57\t    totalCounter += 1;\n58\t    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n59\t\n60\t    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n61\t    if (msg.value > 0) {\n62\t      premium = true;\n63\t    } else {\n64\t      premium = false;\n65\t    }\n66\t\n67\t    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n68\t    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n69\t  }\n70\t\n71\t  /**\n72\t   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n73\t   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n74\t   */\n75\t  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n76\t    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n77\t    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  /**\n81\t   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n82\t   */\n83\t  receive() external payable { }\n84\t}\n85\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"c49c921b-f428-4faa-8177-57c359759065","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:26:15.531Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}\n","numLines":85,"startLine":1,"totalLines":85}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCpUWH3toT5UsAypRnMe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XkkUubSjhYNdobTDjYckot","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n/**\n * @notice Minimal ERC20 interface for the token used to pay tips (USDC).\n * @dev USDC on Base returns `bool` from transfer/transferFrom, so we check it.\n */\ninterface IERC20 {\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256);\n    function decimals() external view returns (uint8);\n}\n\n/**\n * @notice A tip jar that collects ERC20 (USDC) tips together with a short message.\n * @dev Tips are pulled from the tipper with `transferFrom`, so the tipper must\n *      `approve` this contract for at least the tip amount beforehand. Funds are\n *      forwarded straight to the jar `owner`; the contract never custodies them.\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    struct Tip {\n        address from;\n        uint256 amount;\n        string message;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n    }\n\n    // The ERC20 token accepted for tips (USDC on Base).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n    // Recipient of every tip.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    // Full history of tips, newest appended to the end.\n    Tip[] public tips;\n    // Running total tipped, in the token's smallest unit.\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n    // Per-address total tipped, in the token's smallest unit.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error TransferFailed();\n\n    constructor(address _token, address _owner) {\n        token = IERC20(_token);\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip of `amount` token units with an optional `message`.\n     * @dev Requires the caller to have approved this contract for `amount` first.\n     * @param amount Tip amount in the token's smallest unit (USDC has 6 decimals).\n     * @param message Short public note shown in the feed (max 280 chars).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > 280) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        bool ok = token.transferFrom(msg.sender, owner, amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        tips.push(Tip({ from: msg.sender, amount: amount, message: message, timestamp: block.timestamp }));\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded so far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return the most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n     */\n    function recentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            out[i] = tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":102,"cache_read_input_tokens":33865,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":102},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"fe3be583-0903-4928-8553-2ad6bad9bb99","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:30:20.029Z","request_id":"req_011CeCpUVH2WQwDQFHEgZtCg"}

totalTipped;\n    // Per-address total tipped, in the token's smallest unit.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a tip of `amount` token units with an optional `message`.\n     * @dev Requires the caller to have approved this contract for `amount` first.\n     * @param amount Tip amount in the token's smallest unit (USDC has 6 decimals).\n     * @param message Short public note shown in the feed (max 280 chars).\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > 280) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        bool ok = far.\n    function tipCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Return the most recent tips, newest first.\n     * @param count Maximum number of tips to return.\n     */\n    function recentTips(uint256 count) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = tips.length;\n        if (count > total) count = total;\n }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCpXNEhHNbev6cpcwn5o","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LsLWjv9viaNEyKp1D21Dna","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Tiny 6-decimal ERC20 standing in for USDC in unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    string public constant name = \"Mock USD Coin\";\n    string public constant symbol = \"USDC\";\n    uint8 public constant decimals = 6;\n\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        require(balanceOf[from] >= amount, \"balance\");\n        require(allowance[from][msg.sender] >= amount, \"allowance\");\n        allowance[from][msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n    address owner = address(0xBEEF);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipForwardsToOwnerAndRecords() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 5e6);\n        jar.tip(5e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5e6);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testRecentTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 100e6);\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"first\");\n        jar.tip(2e6, \"second\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.recentTips(10);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"second\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"first\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"no approval\");\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":183,"cache_read_input_tokens":37378,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":183},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"ea0e0cdb-e613-4939-b536-92f3a7c3ae20","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:30:54.973Z","request_id":"req_011CeCpXMNrPwkQCjaHVLNfz"}

SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Tiny 6-decimal ERC20 standing in for USDC in unit tests.\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    string public constant name = \"Mock USD Coin\";\n    string public constant symbol = \"USDC\";\n    uint8 public constant decimals = 6;\n\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        require(balanceOf[from] >= amount, \"balance\");\n        require(allowance[from][msg.sender] >= amount, \"allowance\");\n        allowance[from][msg.sender] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        return true;\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n    address owner = address(0xBEEF);\n    address alice = address(0xA11CE);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100e6);\n    }\n\n    function testTipForwardsToOwnerAndRecords() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 5e6);\n        jar.tip(5e6, \"gm\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n\n        (address from, uint256 amount, string memory message,) = jar.tips(0);\n        assertEq(from, alice);\n        assertEq(amount, 5e6);\n        assertEq(message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function testRecentTipsNewestFirst() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 100e6);\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"first\");\n        jar.tip(2e6, \"second\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.recentTips(10);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"second\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"first\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function testRevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(1e6, \"no approval\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { HeartIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/solid\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nconst formatUsdc = (value?: bigint) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"—\" : `${Number(formatUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} USDC`;\n\nconst TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: jarContract } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const jarAddress = useJarAddress();\n\n  const { data: balance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, jarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    try {\n      if (!amount) return 0n;\n      return parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const insufficientBalance = balance !== undefined && parsedAmount > balance;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > allowance;\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a tip amount greater than zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Your USDC balance is too low for that tip.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (needsApproval) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [jarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces a readable notification.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!connectedAddress) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n        <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center gap-4\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm opacity-80\">Connect a wallet to leave a USDC tip and a message.</p>\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: {formatUsdc(balance)}</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className={`input input-bordered w-full ${insufficientBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n          </div>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n            rows={2}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={busy || parsedAmount <= 0n || insufficientBalance}\n          onClick={handleTip}\n        >\n          {busy ? (\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n          ) : (\n            <HeartIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          )}\n          {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n ? \"Approve & Send Tip\" : \"Send Tip\"}\n        </button>\n\n        {jarContract && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs opacity-70 text-center\">\n            Tips are forwarded straight to the jar owner. This app runs on a local Base fork.\n          </p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// Small helper so the form can approve the exact jar address without hardcoding it.\nconst useJarAddress = () => {\n  const { data } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"token\" });\n  // We actually need the TipJar's own address; read it from the deployed info hook instead.\n  return useDeployedTipJarAddress();\n};\n\nconst TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: events, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex justify-center py-10\">\n        <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-lg\" />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!events || events.length === 0) {\n    return <p className=\"text-center opacity-70 py-10\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one in the jar!</p>;\n  }\n\n  // Newest first.\n  const ordered = [...events].reverse();\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 w-full\">\n      {ordered.map((event, i) => {\n        const from = event.args.from as string;\n        const amount = event.args.amount as bigint;\n        const message = event.args.message as string;\n        const timestamp = event.args.timestamp as bigint;\n        return (\n          <li key={`${event.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow\">\n            <div className=\"card-body p-4 gap-2\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                <Address address={from} size=\"sm\" />\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary badge-lg font-semibold\">{formatUsdc(amount)}</span>\n              </div>\n              {message && <p className=\"text-sm break-words\">“{message}”</p>}\n              <span className=\"text-xs opacity-60\">\n                {new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString()}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </ul>\n  );\n};\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const jarAddress = useDeployedTipJarAddress();\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n  const { data: owner } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"text-center max-w-2xl\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n          <HeartIcon className=\"h-8 w-8 text-primary\" />\n          USDC Tip Jar\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-2 opacity-80\">Send USDC tips on Base with a public message. All tips go to the jar owner.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 my-8\">\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips received</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Jar owner</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-base pt-2\">\n            {owner ? <Address address={owner as string} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center\">\n          <TipForm />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <TipFeed />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <p className=\"text-xs opacity-50 mt-10\">\n        Contract {jarAddress ? <Address address={jarAddress} size=\"xs\" /> : null} on {targetNetwork.name} (chain{\" \"}\n        {targetNetwork.id})\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// Reads the deployed TipJar address from the generated contract declaration.\nfunction useDeployedTipJarAddress(): string | undefined {\n  const { data } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  void data;\n  return TIP_JAR_ADDRESS;\n}\n\n// Filled in below from the generated deployedContracts so the UI can approve/link it.\nlet TIP_JAR_ADDRESS: string | undefined;\ntry {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires\n  const deployed = require(\"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\").default;\n  TIP_JAR_ADDRESS = deployed?.[31337]?.TipJar?.address;\n} catch {\n  TIP_JAR_ADDRESS = undefined;\n}\n\nexport default Home;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":227,"cache_read_input_tokens":57512,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":227},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"5679810d-cd8e-4a50-a0ee-5ba40a9985fc","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:34:52.761Z","request_id":"req_011CeCpnGws3iArh87ei62z7"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R3kPWUJAs351JaNTVCggbc","type":"tool_result","content":"export function useDeployedContractInfo<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  config: UseDeployedContractConfig<TContractName>,\n): DeployedContractData<TContractName>;\n/**\n * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n */\nexport function useDeployedContractInfo<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  contractName: TContractName,\n): DeployedContractData<TContractName>;\n\nexport function useDeployedContractInfo<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  configOrName: UseDeployedContractConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n): DeployedContractData<TContractName> {\n  const isMounted = useIsMounted();\n\n  const finalConfig: UseDeployedContractConfig<TContractName> =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\" ? { contractName: configOrName } : (configOrName as any);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useDeployedContractInfo` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n  const { contractName, chainId } = finalConfig;\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n  const deployedContract = contracts?.[selectedNetwork.id]?.[contractName as ContractName] as Contract<TContractName>;\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<ContractCodeStatus>(ContractCodeStatus.LOADING);\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient({ chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const checkContractDeployment = async () => {\n      try {\n        if (!isMounted() || !publicClient) return;\n\n        if (!deployedContract) {\n          setStatus(ContractCodeStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n          return;\n        }\n\n        const code = await publicClient.getCode({\n          address: deployedContract.address,\n        });\n\n        // If contract code is `0x` => no contract deployed on that address\n        if (code === \"0x\") {\n          setStatus(ContractCodeStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n          return;\n        }\n        setStatus(ContractCodeStatus.DEPLOYED);\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error(e);\n        setStatus(ContractCodeStatus.NOT_FOUND);\n      }\n    };\n\n    checkContractDeployment();\n  }, [isMounted, contractName, deployedContract, publicClient]);\n\n  return {\n    data: status === ContractCodeStatus.DEPLOYED ? 6;\n\nconst formatUsdc = (value?: bigint) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"—\" : `${Number(formatUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} USDC`;\n\nconst TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: jarContract } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const jarAddress = useJarAddress();\n\n  const { data: balance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, jarAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeJar } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    try {\n      if (!amount) return 0n;\n      return parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const insufficientBalance = balance !== undefined && parsedAmount > balance;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > allowance;\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a tip amount greater than zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Your USDC balance is too low for that tip.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (needsApproval) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [jarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces a readable notification.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!connectedAddress) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n        <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center gap-4\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm opacity-80\">Connect a wallet to leave a USDC tip and a message.</p>\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: {formatUsdc(balance)}</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className={`input input-bordered w-full ${insufficientBalance ?     <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n          </div>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n            rows={2}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={busy || (\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n          ) : (\n            <HeartIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          )}\n          {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n ? \"Approve & Send Tip\" : \"Send Tip\"}\n        </button>\n\n        {jarContract && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs opacity-70 text-center\">\n            Tips are forwarded straight to the jar owner. {\n      notification.error(\"Your USDC balance is too low for that tip.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (needsApproval) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [jarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces a readable notification.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!connectedAddress) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n        <div className=\"card-body items-center <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body  );\n};\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const jarAddress = useDeployedTipJarAddress();\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = const { data: balance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = try {\n      if (!amount) return 0n;\n      return parseUnits(amount, USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      return 0n;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const insufficientBalance = balance !== undefined &&         </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className={`input input-bordered w-full className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: {formatUsdc(balance)}</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className={`input input-bordered w-full ${insufficientBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n          </div>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n            rows={2}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={busy || parsedAmount <= 0n || insufficientBalance}\n          onClick={handleTip}\n        >\n          {busy ? (\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n          ) : (\n            <HeartIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          )}\n          {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n ? \"Approve & Send Tip\" : \"Send Tip\"}\n        </button>\n\n        <p className=\"text-xs opacity-70 text-center\">\n          Tips are forwarded straight to the jar owner. This app runs on a local Base fork.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nconst TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: events, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex justify-center py-10\">\n        <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-lg\" />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!events || events.length === 0) {\n    return <p className=\"text-center opacity-70 py-10\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one in the jar!</p>;\n  }\n\n  // Newest first.\n  const ordered = [...events].reverse();\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 w-full\">\n      {ordered.map((event, i) => {\n        const from = event.args.from as string;\n        const amount = event.args.amount as bigint;\n        const message = event.args.message as string;\n        const timestamp = event.args.timestamp as bigint;\n        return (\n          <li key={`${event.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow\">\n            <div className=\"card-body p-4 gap-2\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                <Address address={from} size=\"sm\" />\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary badge-lg font-semibold\">{formatUsdc(amount)}</span>\n              </div>\n              {message && <p className=\"text-sm break-words\">“{message}”</p>}\n              <span className=\"text-xs opacity-60\">\n                {new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString()}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </ul>\n  );\n};\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: jarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const jarAddress = contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n\n  return (\n    <div gap-2\">\n          <HeartIcon className=\"h-8 w-8 text-primary\" />\n          USDC Tip Jar\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-2 opacity-80\">Send USDC tips on Base with a public message. All tips go to the jar owner.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 my-8\">\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips received</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Jar owner</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-base pt-2\">\n            {owner ? <Address address={owner as string} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center\">\n          <TipForm jarAddress={jarAddress} />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <TipFeed />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <p className=\"text-xs opacity-50 mt-10\">\n        Contract {jarAddress ? const { data: balance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance } = notification.error(\"Enter a tip amount greater than zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Your USDC balance is too low for that tip.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (needsApproval) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [jarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n zero.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (insufficientBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Your USDC balance is too low for that tip.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      if (needsApproval) {\n        await writeUsdc({\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [jarAddress, parsedAmount],\n        });\n      }\n      await writeJar({\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [parsedAmount, message],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      // useScaffoldWriteContract already surfaces a readable notification.\n      console.error(e);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!connectedAddress) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n        <div className=\"card-body items-center text-center gap-4\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n          <p className=\"text-sm opacity-80\">Connect a wallet to leave a USDC tip and a message.</p>\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">Balance: {formatUsdc(balance)}</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className={`input input-bordered w-full ${insufficientBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">{message.length}/280</span>\n          </div>\n          <textarea\n            className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full\"\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n            rows={2}\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n          disabled={busy || parsedAmount <= 0n || insufficientBalance}\n          onClick={handleTip}\n        >\n          {busy ? (\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />\n          ) : (\n            <HeartIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          )}\n          {needsApproval && parsedAmount > 0n ? \"Approve & Send Tip\" : \"Send Tip\"}\n        </button>\n\n        <p className=\"text-xs opacity-70 text-center\">\n          Tips are forwarded straight to the jar owner. This app runs on a local Base fork.\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nconst TipFeed = () => {\n  const { data: events, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex justify-center py-10\">\n        <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-lg\" />\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!events || events.length === 0) {\n    return <p className=\"text-center opacity-70 py-10\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop one in the jar!</p>;\n  }\n\n  // Newest first.\n  const ordered = [...events].reverse();\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 w-full\">\n      {ordered.map((event, i) => {\n        const from = event.args.from as string;\n        const amount = event.args.amount as bigint;\n        const message = event.args.message as string;\n        const timestamp = event.args.timestamp as bigint;\n        return (\n          <li key={`${event.transactionHash}-${i}`} className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow\">\n            <div className=\"card-body p-4 gap-2\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                <Address address={from} size=\"sm\" />\n                <span className=\"badge badge-primary badge-lg font-semibold\">{formatUsdc(amount)}</span>\n              </div>\n              {message && <p className=\"text-sm break-words\">“{message}”</p>}\n              <span className=\"text-xs opacity-60\">\n                {new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString()}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </li>\n        );\n      })}\n    </ul>\n  );\n};\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: jarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const jarAddress = jarInfo?.address;\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n  const { data: owner } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"text-center max-w-2xl\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n          <HeartIcon className=\"h-8 w-8 text-primary\" />\n          USDC Tip Jar\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-2 opacity-80\">Send USDC tips on Base with a public message. All tips go to the jar owner.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 my-8\">\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips received</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Jar owner</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-base pt-2\">\n            {owner ? <Address address={owner as string} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center\">\n          <TipForm jarAddress={jarAddress} />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <TipFeed />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"text-xs opacity-50 mt-10 flex items-center gap-1\">\n        <span>Contract</span>\n        {jarAddress ? <Address address={jarAddress} size=\"xs\" /> : null}\n        <span>\n          on {targetNetwork.name} (chain {targetNetwork.id})\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Home;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":6,"newStart":1,"newLines":6,"lines":[" \"use client\";"," ","-import { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";","+import { useState } from \"react\";"," import { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";"," import { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";"," import { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019MqtZNQsYv4LHPkyHu1QTS","type":"tool_result","content":"{\n  \"name\": \"@se-2/foundry\",\n  \"version\": \"0.0.1\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"account\": \"make account\",\n    \"account:generate\": \"node scripts-js/generateKeystore.js\",\n    \"account:import\": \"node scripts-js/importAccount.js\",\n    \"account:reveal-pk\": \"node scripts-js/revealPK.js\",\n    \"chain\": \"make chain\",\n    \"clean\": \"forge clean\",\n    \"compile\": \"make compile\",\n    \"deploy\": \"node scripts-js/parseArgs.js\",\n    \"flatten\": \"make flatten\",\n    \"fork\": \"make fork FORK_URL=${1:-mainnet}\",\n    \"format\": \"make format\",\n    \"postinstall\": \"shx cp -n .env.example .env\",\n    \"lint\": \"make lint\",\n    \"test\": \"forge test\",\n    \"verify\": \"make verify RPC_URL=${1:-localhost}\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"dotenv\": \"~16.3.1\",\n    \"envfile\": \"~6.18.0\",\n    \"ethers\": \"~5.7.1\",\n    \"prettier\": \"~2.8.8\",\n    \"qrcode\": \"~1.5.3\",\n    \"toml\": \"~3.0.0\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n=== Makefile fork/chain ===\n.PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCqPhB7pD7kRhj2tLfzv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The `fork:base` command is wired up. Now let me add a small helper script to fund a demo address with USDC (used in the README), then write the README."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":434,"cache_read_input_tokens":95913,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":434},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"c748f93a-05c2-451f-b73a-59ef7f72ddde","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:42:13.813Z","request_id":"req_011CeCqPftEJ694xFL5KcrjR"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCqPhB7pD7kRhj2tLfzv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SfKFdJyB6fX69mGrQPo2hk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/scripts/fund-usdc.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#\n# Fund an address with USDC on the local Base fork by impersonating a whale.\n# Only works against a local Anvil fork (chain id 31337) — it uses cheat RPCs\n# that a real chain does not expose. Nothing here is broadcast to Base.\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <recipient> [amount-in-usdc]\n#   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 250\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\"\n# A Base address that holds millions of USDC in mainnet state.\nWHALE=\"0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\"\n\nRECIPIENT=\"${1:?Usage: fund-usdc.sh <recipient> [amount-in-usdc]}\"\nAMOUNT_USDC=\"${2:-250}\"\n# USDC has 6 decimals.\nAMOUNT=\"$(cast to-wei \"$AMOUNT_USDC\" mwei)\"\n\necho \"Giving the whale some ETH for gas...\"\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$WHALE\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Impersonating whale and sending $AMOUNT_USDC USDC to $RECIPIENT...\"\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$WHALE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \"$RECIPIENT\" \"$AMOUNT\" \\\n  --from \"$WHALE\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$WHALE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\nBAL=\"$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$RECIPIENT\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Done. $RECIPIENT now holds $BAL USDC units (6 decimals).\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":434,"cache_read_input_tokens":95913,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":434},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"f25f0690-41f1-4a4e-a0a6-7f75b3ce1f21","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:42:25.604Z","request_id":"req_011CeCqPftEJ694xFL5KcrjR"}

Nothing here is broadcast to Base.\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <recipient> [amount-in-usdc]\n#   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266 250\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nRPC=\"${RPC_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:8545}\"\nUSDC=\"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\"\n# A Base address that holds millions of USDC in mainnet state.\nWHALE=\"0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\"\n\nRECIPIENT=\"${1:?Usage: fund-usdc.sh <recipient> [amount-in-usdc]}\"\nAMOUNT_USDC=\"${2:-250}\"\n# USDC has 6 decimals.\nAMOUNT=\"$(cast to-wei \"$AMOUNT_USDC\" mwei)\"\n\necho \"Giving the whale some ETH for gas...\"\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance \"$WHALE\" 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\necho \"Impersonating whale and sending $AMOUNT_USDC USDC to $RECIPIENT...\"\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount \"$WHALE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast send \"$USDC\" \"transfer(address,uint256)\" \"$RECIPIENT\" \"$AMOUNT\" \\\n  --from \"$WHALE\" --unlocked --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount \"$WHALE\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\" >/dev/null\n\nBAL=\"$(cast call \"$USDC\" \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" \"$RECIPIENT\" --rpc-url \"$RPC\")\"\necho \"Done. $RECIPIENT now holds $BAL USDC units (6 decimals).\"\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCqRfRkJPKtnWkGwrxvC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NNTAf9rspVmKpJAQSBnBJL","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA minimal onchain tip jar for **USDC on Base**. Anyone can send a USDC tip with a\nshort public message; every tip is forwarded straight to the jar owner and shown\nin a live feed. Built on [Scaffold-ETH 2](https://scaffoldeth.io) (Foundry + Next.js).\n\nThe whole thing runs **locally against a fork of Base mainnet**, so it uses the\nreal Base USDC contract (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) without\nspending real money or deploying anywhere.\n\n## What's in the box\n\n| Piece            | Location                                             | Notes                                                        |\n| ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `TipJar` contract| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`              | Pulls USDC via `transferFrom`, records a feed, emits `NewTip`|\n| Deploy script    | `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol`         | Wires the jar to Base USDC and sets the deployer as owner    |\n| Contract tests   | `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol`                 | Unit tests with a 6-decimal mock USDC                        |\n| Web app          | `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`                       | Tip feed, tip form (approve + tip), connect-wallet flow      |\n| USDC registration| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`     | Minimal ERC-20 ABI so the UI can read balance / approve      |\n\n### How the contract works\n\n`tip(uint256 amount, string message)` pulls `amount` USDC from the caller with\n`transferFrom` (so the caller must `approve` the jar first) and forwards it to the\n`owner`. The contract never custodies funds. It keeps a `tips[]` history plus\n`totalTipped` / `tippedBy` totals and emits `NewTip` for the feed. USDC uses **6\ndecimals**, so 1 USDC = `1_000_000` units.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- [Node.js](https://nodejs.org) >= 20 (tested on 22)\n- [Yarn](https://yarnpkg.com) v3+ (repo is set up for Yarn 4)\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`)\n- Internet access — the local chain forks Base mainnet over its public RPC\n\n## Setup\n\nRun each numbered block in its **own terminal** from the project root\n(`tip-jar/`). Terminals 1–3 stay running.\n\n### 1. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\nyarn install\ngit submodule update --init --recursive   # fetches forge-std\n```\n\n> This project uses a self-contained `IERC20` interface and does **not** require\n> the OpenZeppelin submodule, so no extra Foundry libraries need to be fetched.\n\n### 2. Terminal 1 — start the Base fork\n\n```bash\nyarn fork:base\n```\n\nThis runs Anvil forked from Base mainnet as **chain id 31337**, with 1-second\ninterval mining so `block.timestamp` (and the tip feed's timestamps) keep\nadvancing. Leave it running.\n\n### 3. Terminal 2 — deploy the contract\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nDeploys `TipJar` to the fork and regenerates the frontend ABI at\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The jar owner is Anvil account\n`#9` (`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`).\n\n### 4. Terminal 3 — start the web app\n\n```bash\nyarn start\n```\n\nOpen **http://localhost:3000**. You'll see the tip feed, the tip form, and the\nstats (total tipped, tip count, jar owner).\n\n## Sending a tip locally\n\n1. **Connect a wallet.** On the local network the app offers a built-in *burner\n   wallet* (top-right connect button) — the quickest option. You can also connect\n   MetaMask to the `Localhost 8545` / chain id `31337` network.\n2. **Get some ETH for gas.** Use the **faucet button** in the header (visible on\n   local networks) to send test ETH to your connected address.\n3. **Get some USDC.** Fund your connected address from a Base USDC whale on the\n   fork with the helper script (run from the project root):\n\n   ```bash\n   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <your-connected-address> 250\n   ```\n\n   This impersonates a whale and transfers 250 USDC to you. It only works on the\n   local fork (it uses Anvil cheat RPCs) and broadcasts nothing to Base.\n4. **Send the tip.** Enter an amount and a message and click **Send Tip**. The\n   first tip triggers a USDC `approve` transaction, then the `tip` call. Your tip\n   appears at the top of the feed and the stats update.\n\n## Running the contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test        # or: cd packages/foundry && forge test -vvv\n```\n\nThe tests deploy a small mock USDC, so they run standalone without a fork.\n\n## Notes & design decisions\n\n- **Fork, not a mock token.** The task targets real Base USDC, so the app runs\n  against a Base fork and talks to the canonical USDC contract at the same\n  address. The frontend therefore targets chain id **31337** (the local fork) in\n  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, not Base's live chain id (8453).\n- **USDC returns a bool.** Base USDC returns `bool` from `transferFrom`, so the\n  contract checks the return value and reverts with `TransferFailed` on failure.\n- **No deployment.** Per the task, nothing is deployed to a live network. To\n  target real Base later you'd add the network in `scaffold.config.ts`, register\n  USDC under chain id `8453` in `externalContracts.ts`, and run\n  `yarn deploy --network base` with a funded keystore.\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\ntip-jar/\n├── packages/\n│   ├── foundry/        # Solidity contract, deploy script, tests\n│   └── nextjs/         # Next.js frontend (App Router, wagmi, RainbowKit, DaisyUI)\n└── scripts/\n    └── fund-usdc.sh    # Fund an address with USDC on the local fork\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":260,"cache_read_input_tokens":97904,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":260},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"9e3d2a0d-3cc1-4bfa-a8da-d125e2a41550","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:43:04.503Z","request_id":"req_011CeCqReKGwWCv2Edn8UMKx"}

You'll see the tip feed, the tip form, and the\nstats (total tipped, tip count, jar owner).\n\n## Sending a tip locally\n\n1. **Connect a wallet.** On the local network the app offers a built-in *burner\n   wallet* (top-right connect button) — the quickest option. You can also connect\n   MetaMask to the `Localhost 8545` / chain id `31337` network.\n2. **Get some ETH for gas.** Use the **faucet button** in the header (visible on\n   local networks) to send test ETH to your connected address.\n3. **Get some USDC.** Fund your connected address from a Base USDC whale on the\n   fork with the helper script (run from the project root):\n\n   ```bash\n   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <your-connected-address> 250\n   ```\n\n   This impersonates a whale and transfers 250 USDC to you. It only works on the\n   local fork (it uses Anvil cheat RPCs) and broadcasts nothing to Base.\n4. **Send the tip.** Enter an amount and a message and click **Send Tip**. The\n   first tip triggers a USDC `approve` transaction, then the `tip` call. Your tip\n   appears at the top of the feed and the stats update.\n\n## Running the contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test        # or: cd packages/foundry && forge test -vvv\n```\n\nThe tests deploy a small mock USDC, so they run standalone without a fork.\n\n## Notes & design decisions\n\n- **Fork, not a mock token.** The task targets real Base USDC, so the app runs\n  against a Base fork and talks to the canonical USDC contract at the same\n  address.
