The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.","+This runs Anvil forked from Base mainnet as **chain id 31337**, with 1-second","+interval mining so `block.timestamp` (and the tip feed's timestamps) keep","+advancing. Leave it running."," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","+### 3. Terminal 2 — deploy the contract"," ","-```","+```bash"," yarn deploy"," ```"," ","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","+Deploys `TipJar` to the fork and regenerates the frontend ABI at","+`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The jar owner is Anvil account","+`#9` (`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`)."," ","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","+### 4. Terminal 3 — start the web app"," ","-```","+```bash"," yarn start"," ```"," ","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+Open **http://localhost:3000**. You'll see the tip feed, the tip form, and the","+stats (total tipped, tip count, jar owner)."," ","-Run smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`","+## Sending a tip locally"," ","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`","+1. **Connect a wallet.** On the local network the app offers a built-in *burner","+   wallet* (top-right connect button) — the quickest option. You can also connect","+   MetaMask to the `Localhost 8545` / chain id `31337` network.","+2. **Get some ETH for gas.** Use the **faucet button** in the header (visible on","+   local networks) to send test ETH to your connected address.","+3. **Get some USDC.** Fund your connected address from a Base USDC whale on the","+   fork with the helper script (run from the project root):"," ","+   ```bash","+   ./scripts/fund-usdc.sh <your-connected-address> 250","+   ```"," ","-## Documentation","+   This impersonates a whale and transfers 250 USDC to you. It only works on the","+   local fork (it uses Anvil cheat RPCs) and broadcasts nothing to Base.","+4. **Send the tip.** Enter an amount and a message and click **Send Tip**. The","+   first tip triggers a USDC `approve` transaction, then the `tip` call. Your tip","+   appears at the top of the feed and the stats update."," ","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","+## Running the contract tests"," ","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","+```bash","+yarn foundry:test        # or: cd packages/foundry && forge test -vvv","+```"," ","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","+The tests deploy a small mock USDC, so they run standalone without a fork."," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+## Notes & design decisions"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+- **Fork, not a mock token.** The task targets real Base USDC, so the app runs","+  against a Base fork and talks to the canonical USDC contract at the same","+  address. The frontend therefore targets chain id **31337** (the local fork) in","+  `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`, not Base's live chain id (8453).","+- **USDC returns a bool.** Base USDC returns `bool` from `transferFrom`, so the","+  contract checks the return value and reverts with `TransferFailed` on failure.","+- **No deployment.** Per the task, nothing is deployed to a live network. To","+  target real Base later you'd add the network in `scaffold.config.ts`, register","+  USDC under chain id `8453` in `externalContracts.ts`, and run","+  `yarn deploy --network base` with a funded keystore.","+","+## Project layout","+","+```","+tip-jar/","+├── packages/","+│   ├── foundry/        # Solidity contract, deploy script, tests","+│   └── nextjs/         # Next.js frontend (App Router, wagmi, RainbowKit, DaisyUI)","+└── scripts/","+    └── fund-usdc.sh    # Fund an address with USDC on the local fork","+```"]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HyQn9xrgmTiYFSvkAKVUrb","type":"tool_result","content":"203\tconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n204\t  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n205\t  const { data: jarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n206\t  const jarAddress = jarInfo?.address;\n207\t\n208\t  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n209\t  const { data: owner } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n210\t  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n211\t\n212\t  return (\n213\t    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n214\t      <div className=\"text-center max-w-2xl\">\n215\t        <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n216\t          <HeartIcon className=\"h-8 w-8 text-primary\" />\n217\t          USDC Tip Jar\n218\t        </h1>\n219\t        <p className=\"mt-2 opacity-80\">Send USDC tips on Base with a public message. All tips go to the jar owner.</p>\n220\t      </div>\n221\t\n222\t      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 my-8\">\n223\t        <div className=\"stat\">\n224\t          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n225\t          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n226\t        </div>\n227\t        <div className=\"stat\">\n228\t          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips received</div>\n229\t          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n230\t        </div>\n231\t        <div className=\"stat\">\n232\t          <div className=\"stat-title\">Jar owner</div>\n233\t          <div className=\"stat-value text-base pt-2\">\n234\t            {owner ? <Address address={owner as string} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n235\t          </div>\n236\t        </div>\n237\t      </div>\n238\t\n239\t      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n240\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center\">\n241\t          <TipForm jarAddress={jarAddress} />\n242\t        </div>\n243\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n244\t          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Tip feed</h2>\n245\t          <TipFeed />\n246\t        </div>\n247\t      </div>\n248\t\n249\t      <div className=\"text-xs opacity-50 mt-10 flex items-center gap-1\">\n250\t        <span>Contract</span>\n251\t        {jarAddress ? <Address address={jarAddress} size=\"xs\" /> : null}\n252\t        <span>\n253\t          on {targetNetwork.name} (chain {targetNetwork.id})\n254\t        </span>\n255\t      </div>\n256\t    </div>\n257\t  );\n258\t};\n259\t\n260\texport default Home;\n261\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"0b5f5210-5b56-4d6f-9871-effab511d322","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:43:14.320Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-19T212323Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/tip-jar/packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx","content":"const Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: jarInfo } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n  const jarAddress = jarInfo?.address;\n\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n  const { data: owner } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"text-center max-w-2xl\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-4xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n          <HeartIcon className=\"h-8 w-8 text-primary\" />\n          USDC Tip Jar\n        </h1>\n        <p className=\"mt-2 opacity-80\">Send USDC tips on Base with a public message. All tips go to the jar owner.</p>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"stats stats-vertical sm:stats-horizontal shadow bg-base-100 my-8\">\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-primary text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips received</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"stat\">\n          <div className=\"stat-title\">Jar owner</div>\n          <div className=\"stat-value text-base pt-2\">\n            {owner ? <Address address={owner as string} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 w-full max-w-4xl\">\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center\">\n          <TipForm jarAddress={jarAddress} />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n          <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-bold\">Tip feed</h2>\n          <TipFeed />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"text-xs opacity-50 mt-10 flex items-center gap-1\">\n        <span>Contract</span>\n        {jarAddress ? <Address address={jarAddress} size=\"xs\" /> : null}\n        <span>\n          on {targetNetwork.name} (chain {targetNetwork.id})\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Home;\n","numLines":59,"startLine":203,"totalLines":261}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeCqYi3D1ZUoQdJtoEjpL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Both services are live. The USDC tip jar is built and running locally end-to-end.\n\n## What's running\n\n- **Base fork** — Anvil forked from Base mainnet as chain `31337` with 1s interval mining (so feed timestamps stay live), at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n- **Frontend** — Next.js at `http://localhost:3000` (200 OK).\n- **Contract** — `TipJar` deployed to the fork, wired to real Base USDC (`0x8335…2913`), owner = Anvil account #9.\n\n## What I built\n\n**Contract** (`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`) — `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `transferFrom` (so the tipper approves first), forwards it straight to the owner (never custodies), records a `tips[]` feed with totals, and emits `NewTip`. Uses a self-contained `IERC20` and checks USDC's `bool` return. 4 Foundry tests pass against a mock USDC.\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`) — tip feed (live `NewTip` events, newest first), a tip form that runs the approve→tip flow with balance/allowance checks, live stats (total tipped / count / owner), and the built-in connect-wallet + faucet flow. USDC is registered in `externalContracts.ts` (chain 31337) so scaffold hooks drive approve/balance.\n\n**Tooling** — `yarn fork:base` (one-command Base fork), and `scripts/fund-usdc.sh` to fund any address with USDC from a whale on the fork.\n\n## Verified end-to-end\n\nAgainst the running fork I funded an account with real USDC from a whale, approved, and tipped 25 USDC — `totalTipped`, `tipCount`, `recentTips`, and the owner's USDC balance all updated correctly, and the `NewTip` event carried the message. Contract force-build clean, all tests pass, frontend lint/types clean (the one remaining warning is just \"`useScaffoldEventHistory` is recommended for local chains\" — exactly our case).\n\n## Notes\n\n- Per the task, **nothing is deployed** anywhere — the deliverable is the local project + `README.md`, which documents prerequisites, the 3-terminal setup (`yarn install` → `yarn fork:base` → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`), and how to get test ETH/USDC and send a tip.\n- The frontend targets chain `31337` (the local fork), not Base's live `8453` — the README explains what to change for a real Base deploy later.\n- I skipped OpenZeppelin (its submodule wouldn't fetch in this environment and isn't needed) and removed the unused `VerifyAll.s.sol` verification script since we don't deploy.\n\nOne heads-up: I left the earlier test tip (25 USDC) in the jar state so the feed isn't empty on first open. If you'd prefer a clean slate, restart `yarn fork:base` and re-run `yarn deploy`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":813,"cache_read_input_tokens":104771,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":813},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6734efc9-4f82-458f-8294-43d27afb8d9e","uuid":"bd9478af-dd03-45f8-9c16-0a25142fd625","timestamp":"2026-08-19T21:44:22.576Z","request_id":"req_011CeCqYgzx7hcbgJyDmBUQ3"}

The USDC tip jar is built and running locally end-to-end.\n\n## What's running\n\n- **Base fork** — Anvil forked from Base mainnet as chain `31337` with 1s interval mining (so feed timestamps stay live), at `http://127.0.0.1:8545`.\n- **Frontend** — Next.js at `http://localhost:3000` (200 OK).\n- **Contract** — `TipJar` deployed to the fork, wired to real Base USDC (`0x8335…2913`), owner = Anvil account #9.\n\n## What I built\n\n**Contract** (`packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`) — `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `transferFrom` (so the tipper approves first), forwards it straight to the owner (never custodies), records a `tips[]` feed with totals, and emits `NewTip`. Uses a self-contained `IERC20` and checks USDC's `bool` return. 4 Foundry tests pass against a mock USDC.\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`) — tip feed (live `NewTip` events, newest first), a tip form that runs the approve→tip flow with balance/allowance checks, live stats (total tipped / count / owner), and the built-in connect-wallet + faucet flow. USDC is registered in `externalContracts.ts` (chain 31337) so scaffold hooks drive approve/balance.\n\n**Tooling** — `yarn fork:base` (one-command Base fork), and `scripts/fund-usdc.sh` to fund any address with USDC from a whale on the fork.\n\n## Verified end-to-end\n\nAgainst the running fork I funded an account with real USDC from a whale, approved, and tipped 25 USDC — `totalTipped`, `tipCount`, `recentTips`, and the owner's USDC balance all updated correctly, and the `NewTip` event carried the message. Contract force-build clean, all tests pass, frontend lint/types clean (the one remaining warning is just \"`useScaffoldEventHistory` is recommended for local chains\" — exactly our case).\n\n## Notes\n\n- Per the task, **nothing is deployed** anywhere — the deliverable is the local project + `README.md`, which documents prerequisites, the 3-terminal setup (`yarn install` → `yarn fork:base` → `yarn deploy` → `yarn start`), and how to get test ETH/USDC and send a tip.\n- The frontend targets chain `31337` (the local fork), not Base's live `8453` — the README explains what to change for a real Base deploy later.\n- I skipped OpenZeppelin (its submodule wouldn't fetch in this environment and isn't needed) and removed the unused `VerifyAll.s.sol` verification script since we don't deploy.\n\nOne heads-up: I left the earlier test tip (25 USDC) in the jar state so the feed isn't empty on first open.
